# yt-dlp equivalence matrix

[Home](../Home.md) · [ADR-011](../03-decisions/ADR-011-X-twitter-phase.md) · [ADR-009](../03-decisions/ADR-009-Media-toolkit-phase.md) · [ADR-008](../03-decisions/ADR-008-Extractor-core-and-youtube-phase.md) · [Phase 7](../00-project/Phase-7-X-Twitter.md) · [Phase 5](../00-project/Phase-5-Media-Toolkit.md) · [MeTube parity](Feature-parity.md)

**Baseline:** yt-dlp tag `2026.08.19`, commit `3a08beaf031ab68f966401ead017ac81fe8486cf`, inspected 2026-09-24. This note is the target for "the Kotlin port of yt-dlp" from [ADR-004](../03-decisions/ADR-004-Local-kotlin-engine.md). The [MeTube matrix](Feature-parity.md) covers the product workflows on top; this one covers the engine underneath.

Two halves, decided by the owner on 2026-09-24 ([ADR-008](../03-decisions/ADR-008-Extractor-core-and-youtube-phase.md)):

1. **Core engine** — the hand-written table below. A row is *Done* only when the owning task links tests on every host the row applies to.
2. **Extractor catalog** — 1,751 extractor classes named in upstream `_extractors.py` at the pin. Coverage is a generated table from `port/manifest.json` ([T-055](../06-tasks/T-055-Port-manifest-and-equivalence-matrix.md)). Nobody edits that table by hand.

Statuses: **Done** (tested on every applicable host), **Partial** (subset shipped; the note says which), **D7** (in the current phase), **Later** (planned, no phase), **Out** (deliberately not equivalent; the reason is in the row).

## Core engine rows

| ID | yt-dlp capability (upstream module) | Kotlin target and honest scope | Status | Tasks |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| E-01 | URL matching and extractor dispatch (`extractor/common.py` `_VALID_URL`, `suitable`, `_match_id`; `extractor/_extractors.py`) | `ExtractorRegistry` picks the first suitable `InfoExtractor`; generic subset is last resort. | Done | [T-057](../06-tasks/T-057-Extractor-core.md) |
| E-02 | `info_dict` and format model (`common.py` `_real_extract` contract, `formats`, `ext`/`vcodec`/`acodec`/`tbr`/`height`/`fps`/`protocol`/`filesize`) | `InfoDict` and `MediaFormat` with the upstream field names in Kotlin case. Fields the port does not fill stay null. | Done | T-057 |
| E-03 | Extractor helper library (`_search_regex`, `_parse_json`, `traverse_obj`, `url_or_none`, `int_or_none`, `unescapeHTML`, `_download_json`/`_download_webpage`) | Subset translated as needed by each extractor, recorded in the manifest as `utils`/`common.py` partial. T-136 adds `js_to_json` (scoped: comments, quotes, unquoted keys, trailing commas, hex/octal, `new Date`/`new X`, `parseInt`), `determine_ext`, and JSON-LD object discovery (`_yield_json_ld` plus the `@graph` walk of `_json_ld`). The `_json_ld` VideoObject/interaction/chapter mapping and the rest of both modules stay unported. | Partial | T-057, [T-136](../06-tasks/T-136-Generic-helpers.md) |
| E-04 | Typed extraction errors (`ExtractorError`, `GeoRestrictedError`, login-required, age-limit, unavailable) | Sealed `ExtractionError` mapped to `JobErrorCode`. | Done | T-057, [T-061](../06-tasks/T-061-Engine-extracts-selects-downloads.md) |
| E-05 | Format selection language (`YoutubeDL.build_format_selector`: `best`, `bv*+ba/b`, `[height<=720]`, `/` fallback, `,` multi) | Parser and evaluator cover the upstream language: best/worst/type/`*`/`.N`, filters, `/` fallback, `,` lists that keep every child selection, `all` best-first, `mergeall` over every usable stream, `+` products, and `()` groups; DRM formats are always dropped. On a merge-capable host the compiler emits one `bv*+ba/b` merge with the D4 single-file fallback; typed options compile to a spec and the domain never carries one. The engine still consumes the first selection: a multi-stream `MergeAll` is selected but resolves to a typed “more than two streams” refusal, so multi-stream execution stays out (T-133, T-077). | Partial | [T-058](../06-tasks/T-058-Format-selector.md), [T-077](../06-tasks/T-077-Engine-executes-a-merge.md), [T-133](../06-tasks/T-133-Format-lists.md) |
| E-06 | Format sorting (`FormatSorter`, `-S`: `res,fps,codec,br,size,proto,ext,...`) | Sort fields ported; defaults match upstream. | Done | T-058 |
| E-07 | HTTP downloader (`downloader/http.py`: ranges, `http_chunk_size`, resume, retries) | Ranged chunk download when the format declares a chunk size. T-134 adds same-attempt resume: a body that drops mid-transfer continues from the temp size when the server answers 206 with a matching `Content-Range`, bounded per attempt; a 200 or a mismatched range fails typed and never appends. A relaunch still marks an active job `FAILED`/`ENGINE_UNAVAILABLE` and no byte is reused across launches. Extractor sort fields, proxies, and cross-attempt resume stay out. | Partial | [T-056](../06-tasks/T-056-Http-request-port.md), T-061, [T-134](../06-tasks/T-134-Resume-attempt.md) |
| E-08 | Native HLS downloader (`downloader/hls.py`, `extractor/common._extract_m3u8_formats`) | Media playlist parse, fragment concat, AES-128, fMP4 init; no FFmpeg. Live playlists later. | Partial | [T-073](../06-tasks/T-073-Native-hls-dash-downloaders.md) |
| E-09 | Native DASH downloader (`downloader/dash.py`, `_parse_mpd_formats`) | `SegmentTemplate`/`SegmentList`/`SegmentBase` and fragment concat; no FFmpeg. Live and DRM fail typed. | Partial | T-073 |
| E-10 | Fragment downloader shared logic (`downloader/fragment.py`: concurrency, skip unavailable, retries) | T-135 adds a bounded concurrent window (default 4; `1` restores strict sequence) with ordered writes and AES-128 decryption at each fragment's turn. Skip-unavailable defaults to upstream's VOD `true`, records skipped indexes in the outcome, and fails typed when off; cancellation stops new requests and the caller discards the temp. Retries stay bounded. | Partial | T-073, [T-135](../06-tasks/T-135-Fragment-concurrency.md) |
| E-11 | JS challenge solving (`extractor/youtube/jsc`, yt-dlp-ejs, `n`/`sig`) | `JsRuntime` port; bundled EJS `0.8.0`; QuickJS 2021-03-27 on JVM/Android/iOS; page JS on web. iOS live YouTube remains blocked. T-124 wires the solver into the single-video web client and the PO-token gate. | Done | [T-069](../06-tasks/T-069-Ejs-bundle-and-jsruntime-port.md), [T-070](../06-tasks/T-070-Ejs-challenge-solving-web-client.md), [T-071](../06-tasks/T-071-Js-runtime-desktop-android-ios.md), [T-072](../06-tasks/T-072-Js-runtime-web-page.md), [T-124](../06-tasks/T-124-Youtube-done.md) |
| E-12 | Legacy JS interpreter (`jsinterp.py`) | Not ported. EJS replaces it. | Out | — |
| E-13 | PO token providers (`extractor/youtube/pot`) | T-124 adds the `PoTokenProvider` request/response interface and the default no-token provider: the PLAYER token rides `serviceIntegrityDimensions`, and web-client GVS-gated formats are dropped and counted (`formatsNeedingPoToken`) until a provider is configured. The plugin director, policy table, and token minting stay unported. | Partial | [T-124](../06-tasks/T-124-Youtube-done.md) |
| E-14 | Playlists and entries (`playlist_result`, `--playlist-items`, `--flat-playlist`) | Playlist entries become bounded child jobs (cap 50; limit 0 means 50; failed and duplicate entries follow T-107). The YouTube `/playlist?list=` subset parses flat id/title rows from a redacted browse fixture, follows continuation pages in order (bounded at 10) up to the cap, and children download through `YoutubeIE`. T-124 adds the `--playlist-items` selection (1-based ranges, negatives, steps; invalid specs fail the job typed), `/channel/<UC id>` and `/@handle` tabs through the grid renderers, and `list=RD...` mixes through the playlist-panel renderer. `/c/`, `/user/`, feeds, and live stay partial. | Partial | [T-012](../06-tasks/T-012-Playlists-and-batches.md), [T-107](../06-tasks/T-107-Playlist-expansion.md), [T-108](../06-tasks/T-108-Youtube-playlist-subset.md), [T-124](../06-tasks/T-124-Youtube-done.md) |
| E-15 | Output template (`--output`, `prepare_filename`, sanitization) | Safe title + extension naming, ` (2)`/` (3)` collision suffixes, and path-escape rejection. The full yt-dlp template language stays out. | Partial | [T-014](../06-tasks/T-014-Storage-and-delivery.md), [T-110](../06-tasks/T-110-History-names-folders.md) |
| E-16 | Cookies (`--cookies`, `--cookies-from-browser`, `cookies.py`) | Shared `CookieJar` parses the Netscape file (1 MiB cap; leading `# Netscape HTTP Cookie File` / `# HTTP Cookie File` or a 7-field tab row) and matches host, path, secure, and expiry in commonMain. A `useCookies` job snapshots the jar once and attaches it to extractor, media, manifest, resume, and fragment requests through the trusted `HttpRequest.cookie` field added after `HttpHeaders.sanitize`; a missing, empty, or fully expired file fails typed with no names or values. Desktop, Android, and iOS store one app-private `cookies.txt` with no app-level cipher (Android Auto Backup and iOS backup excluded) and show Not configured / Configured / Error with replace and delete. Desktop can copy Chrome, Firefox, or Safari once after a separate consent screen; Windows Chrome DPAPI and other browsers are recorded gaps, and tests use synthetic stores only. Android, iOS, and web never read a browser database; web records a gap and the page never receives cookie bytes. Unmatched desktop URLs keep `yt-dlp --cookies`, and Android keeps Chaquopy. Cookies do not guarantee access or authorize an unpermitted download. | Done | [T-018](../06-tasks/T-018-Cookie-lifecycle.md) |
| E-17 | Postprocessors: merge (`FFmpegMergerPP`), extract audio, convert, embed thumbnail/metadata/subtitles | D5 merges one video stream with one audio stream and copies or converts audio. Desktop uses FFmpeg on `PATH` (M4A/Opus copy; MP3/WAV/FLAC transcode, `ffprobe`-checked). Android uses MediaMuxer (merge; M4A/Opus copy). iOS uses AVFoundation (merge; M4A copy, Opus to CAF). Web stays a gap. T-015 adds desktop audio tag/artwork embedding and SRT subtitle muxing (`mov_text`/`srt` by container); Android and iOS keep their merge/audio limits and write sidecars instead. Clips and SponsorBlock are T-016. | Partial | [T-075](../06-tasks/T-075-Media-toolkit-contract.md), [T-077](../06-tasks/T-077-Engine-executes-a-merge.md), [T-080](../06-tasks/T-080-Android-mediamuxer.md), [T-081](../06-tasks/T-081-Ios-avfoundation.md), [T-082](../06-tasks/T-082-Audio-containers.md), [T-013](../06-tasks/T-013-Media-formats.md), [T-015](../06-tasks/T-015-Captions-thumbnails-metadata.md) |
| E-18 | Subtitles (`--write-subs`, `--sub-langs`, conversion) | T-124 records manual and automatic caption tracks on the info dict. T-015 selects by language (exact or prefix) and manual/automatic preference, converts `json3`/`srv1-3`/`ttml`/`vtt` to SRT, VTT, TTML, and TXT in pure Kotlin, and publishes sidecars on every host; desktop FFmpeg additionally muxes SRT into the media (`mov_text` for MP4/MOV, `srt` for MKV/WebM), while Android, iOS, and web write sidecars only. A requested language with no match writes nothing and is reported truthfully. | Partial | [T-015](../06-tasks/T-015-Captions-thumbnails-metadata.md), [T-124](../06-tasks/T-124-Youtube-done.md) |
| E-19 | Thumbnails (`--write-thumbnail`, conversion) | T-015 fetches the largest info-dict thumbnail and publishes it as a sidecar with its real extension (jpg/jpeg/png/webp); artwork embedding rides the audio tag capability and is desktop-only in this phase. Server-side conversion to JPG stays out. | Partial | [T-015](../06-tasks/T-015-Captions-thumbnails-metadata.md) |
| E-20 | Chapters, SponsorBlock, `--download-sections` | T-124 records chapter markers on the info dict. T-016 adds validated clip ranges (`HH:MM:SS.mmm`/`MM:SS`/seconds; URL `t`/`start` fallback with an explicit field winning; typed invalid-range errors), chapter split with confined templates and one `CHAPTER` artifact per chapter (size plus the existing History open/download actions), and opt-in SponsorBlock through the external `sponsor.ajay.app` service with typed `removed`/`no-segments`/`unavailable` outcomes recorded on the job. Desktop FFmpeg cuts with `-ss`/`-t -c copy` (nearest keyframe; documented) and removes segments by re-encode; hosts without the capability fail typed. Defined interactions: a clip wins over a split, and SponsorBlock cannot combine with either in this phase. | Partial | [T-016](../06-tasks/T-016-Clips-chapters-SponsorBlock.md), [T-124](../06-tasks/T-124-Youtube-done.md) |
| E-21 | Live streams (`--live-from-start`, HLS live) | T-124 adds live YouTube extraction (on-air videos expose their HLS manifest as an `m3u8_native` format and set `isLive`; was-live videos keep recorded formats) and the engine's live-HLS follower (sliding window, appends each media sequence once, stops on `#EXT-X-ENDLIST` or cancel, bounded polls). `--live-from-start` and DASH live stay out. | Partial | [T-124](../06-tasks/T-124-Youtube-done.md) |
| E-22 | Network: proxies, `--source-address`, rate limit, sleep, retries | Bounded retries in D4. T-017 adds global Settings fields for a proxy URL, a KiB/s rate limit, and sleep-requests/sleep-interval; the Kotlin engine honors the rate limit (chunk pacing) and sleeps before media and fragment requests. A per-job free-form proxy string is rejected: `DownloadOptions` has no proxy field and presets cannot set one (Q-09). `--source-address` stays out; reading the global proxy in the Kotlin transfer adapters is partial (the desktop CLI maps it). | Partial | [T-017](../06-tasks/T-017-Options-and-presets.md) |
| E-23 | Impersonation (`curl_cffi`, `--impersonate`) | Platform HTTP stacks do not impersonate. Recorded gap. | Out | — |
| E-24 | Options surface and config files (`--config-locations`, free-form options, plugins, exec hooks, external downloaders) | Typed allowlist only. Plugins, exec, config files, and external downloaders are out (Q-09, ADR-006). | Out | [T-017](../06-tasks/T-017-Options-and-presets.md) |
| E-25 | Download archive and history (`--download-archive`) | T-017 adds `DownloadArchive` with the upstream `<extractor> <id>` line, engine skip-on-repeat and record-on-success, and a file-backed `FileDownloadArchive` beside desktop app state. Android and iOS file wiring stay partial; the in-app history remains the user-facing archive. | Partial | [T-017](../06-tasks/T-017-Options-and-presets.md), [T-014](../06-tasks/T-014-Storage-and-delivery.md) |
| E-26 | Engine version and update (`--update`, `--version`) | T-017 shows the pinned upstream tag `2026.08.19` and the running Kotlin version in Settings; there is no self-update, update check, or runtime version lookup. | Done | [T-017](../06-tasks/T-017-Options-and-presets.md) |
| E-27 | Extractor test contract (`_TESTS`, `test/test_download.py` matchers: `md5:`, `int`, `str`, `re:`, `only_matching`) | Harness runs upstream-style cases from Kotlin fixtures; live opt-in. | Done | [T-059](../06-tasks/T-059-Extractor-test-harness.md) |
| E-28 | Differential check against upstream | Opt-in `yt-dlp -J` oracle on desktop compares normalized fields. | Done | [T-062](../06-tasks/T-062-Desktop-ytdlp-oracle.md) |

## Extractor coverage

Machine-readable record: `port/manifest.json` lists every translated module with its upstream path, tag, commit, status, scope, and Kotlin files. `port/upstream-extractors.json` is the sorted class-name list read from upstream `_extractors.py` at the same pin (data only, no upstream source). Regenerate the block below with `./gradlew :tools:port-manifest:run`; validate the manifest and the block in CI with `./gradlew :tools:port-manifest:check`. Do not edit the block between the markers by hand.

Counting rule: an extractor is **Ported** when its `_real_extract` behavior for the URL forms listed in the manifest is translated and its harness cases pass; **Partial** when a named subset is translated (the manifest names it); **Planned** when a task exists; otherwise **Not started**. A count is never rounded up.

A task that ports a module updates `port/manifest.json` in the same change and reruns the generator; the coverage counts and the module table below follow from that file alone. The first generation seeded the D3 generic subset as partial ([T-045](../06-tasks/T-045-Generic-extractor-subset.md)), the YouTube single video as planned ([T-060](../06-tasks/T-060-Youtube-jsless-extractor.md)), and D7 added `TwitterIE` partial ([T-094](../06-tasks/T-094-Twitter-status-extractor.md)).

The embeddable player's `<play-av playlist=…>` JSON demarks the playlist entries and subtitle tracks; the public `metadata/<identifier>` API supplies item metadata, files, thumbnails, and formats. A single entry becomes the main info dict with `formats`; a multi-entry item becomes bounded child-job entries addressed by `/details/<identifier>/<entry>` URLs. Unknown extensions and private files (no archive.org sign-in) are skipped. `YoutubeWebArchiveIE` is planned under T-124; reviews and the Mixch host are not translated. A login wall or DRM wall is Partial. | [`ArchiveOrgIE.kt`](../../shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/com/anydownload/core/extract/archiveorg/ArchiveOrgIE.kt) | T-125 | 2026-09-30 |

## Named priorities after YouTube

Owner, 2026-09-24: **X / Twitter** (`extractor/twitter.py`, 1,785 lines at the pin) is the next named site. Owner, 2026-09-25: the phase after D4 is the media toolkit ([Phase 5](../00-project/Phase-5-Media-Toolkit.md)), and X/Twitter follows that. **D7 ported `TwitterIE` partial** ([Phase 7](../00-project/Phase-7-X-Twitter.md), [ADR-011](../03-decisions/ADR-011-X-twitter-phase.md), [T-094](../06-tasks/T-094-Twitter-status-extractor.md)): the public single-status guest lookup, videos grouped by stable media id, typed photo-only/protected/deleted failures, and selection download on all four hosts. Photos, threads and quoted posts, cards, Spaces, broadcasts, profiles, `t.co`, cookies, and the GraphQL/legacy API stay out of that partial. Owner, 2026-09-29: the rest of the pin, including the Twitter remainder and the other 1,746 classes, is phased in [ADR-014](../03-decisions/ADR-014-Full-kotlin-engine-schedule.md) and the [full engine schedule](../00-project/Full-engine-schedule.md). D12 finishes YouTube. D16 finishes Twitter and the high-traffic files. D17–D21 take the rest of the catalog. D22 removes the yt-dlp and Chaquopy fallbacks.

## Differential oracle (E-28)

The desktop differential compares the Kotlin extractor with the installed upstream tool on the same public URL. It runs only when explicitly enabled and never in default CI:

```sh
./gradlew :apps:desktop:test --tests "com.anydownlod.desktop.engine.YtDlpOracleTest" -PytDlpOracle=true
```

- Preconditions: `yt-dlp` on `PATH` at the pin `2026.08.19`. Without the property, or with any other version, the test is skipped and reports the version it found. The executable is started with an argument list, never a shell string; `ProcessBuilder` stays under `apps/desktop`. The oracle runs `yt-dlp -J --no-warnings --extractor-args "youtube:player_client=visionos" <url>` so both sides use the same innertube client.
- Compared fields: `id`, `title`, `channel_id`, `upload_date`, `age_limit`, `duration` (±1s), and the format set keyed by `format_id` with `ext`, `vcodec`, `acodec` (a missing codec equals `none`), `height`, `fps` (±0.001), and `protocol`. Ignored: URLs, `filesize`, `view_count`, thumbnails, and storyboard-only rows. The raw `-J` document contains signed media URLs, so it is parsed and discarded, never printed or stored.
- A divergence is a failure whose message lists field names and normalized values only; a `googlevideo` host in the diff fails the test explicitly.
- Allowed inputs live in `YtDlpOracleTest`: the public Big Buck Bunny URL plus its audio-only formats. Add cases there, not to CI.
- First run (2026-09-24) found real gaps in the T-060 translation: `isDrc`/`xtags` format-id suffixes (`-drc`, `-sr`) and the watch-page `uploadDate`/`familyFriendly` fallbacks. After fixing them the differential reports `27/27` formats and `0` diffs.

## How to use this matrix

1. Keep E-IDs stable. Add rows when upstream gains a capability the port must answer; mark rows Out with a reason rather than deleting them.
2. A task that ports a module updates `port/manifest.json` in the same change and regenerates the coverage block.
3. Statuses change only with linked evidence in the owning task. "Compiles" is not evidence; a harness or host test is.
4. The pin moves only at a phase boundary and is recorded in the ADR for that phase, the manifest, and every notice header the change touches.
